# Card review sheet

Generated 2026-10-25 by `npm run cards:review`. Do not edit by hand — edit the
source modules under `src/content` and regenerate. Each row is one card;
the Review column is intentionally blank so you can mark it up.

| ☐ | Two Paths | `pokemon.route-two-paths` | group | A short path through tall grass, or a long one where a trainer wants a fight. |  |
| ☐ | Rare Candy | `pokemon.route-rare-candy-1` | group | A Rare Candy on the ground, a little linty. Your starter is staring at it. |  |

| ☐ | Day Care | `pokemon.route-day-care` | group | The Day Care couple trained your starter while you napped. It looks different. |  |

407 cards · 36 sip · 44 group · 0 category · 303 challenge · 24 rule · 261 dice

| ☐ | Lost and Found | `dungeon.lost-and-found` | sip | Everything anyone lost down here, plus a coaster that looks bulletproof. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Tip Jar | `dungeon.tip-jar` | sip | A tip jar lost under the shelves since 2011. A loaded die rattles in the coins. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Rat Fence | `dungeon.rat-fence` | sip | A rat in a tiny vest is selling whatever fell behind the bar. He takes drinks, or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Old Regular | `dungeon.the-regular` | sip | He's been down here since the cellar was a bar. He'll trade a story for a story. |  |
| ☐ | Cellar Master's Coat | `dungeon.cellar-masters-coat` | sip | A coat on a hook with full pockets: a corkscrew and a smoke bomb. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Magnum | `dungeon.the-magnum` | sip | A magnum of something nice behind the racks. Drink 1, and cork the rest for later. |  |
| ☐ | The Cellar Cat | `dungeon.cellar-cat` | sip | A cat in a sommelier's chain trades mouse-bitten goods. It takes drinks, or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Vintner | `dungeon.the-vintner` | sip | A tiny man in a huge hat sells you a vintage. He insists you taste first. |  |
| ☐ | The Bone Pile | `dungeon.bone-pile` | sip | Under the bones: a smoke bomb and a stranger's fake ID. Keep both. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Offering Bowl | `dungeon.offering-bowl` | sip | A bowl of coins left for the dead, and a draught nobody touched. Take it. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Grave Robber | `dungeon.grave-robber` | sip | A grave robber with a cart of stolen goods and no questions asked. Prices are firm. |  |
| ☐ | The Undertaker | `dungeon.the-undertaker` | sip | He measures you for a coffin while he sells you things. He's very thorough. |  |
| ☐ | Flushed Purse | `dungeon.flushed-purse` | sip | A purse dropped down a toilet, somehow dry: lipstick, a lighter, an open bar tab. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Maintenance Locker | `dungeon.maintenance-locker` | sip | A hard hat on a hook, and a thermos of something brown. Drink 1 from the thermos. |  |
| ☐ | The Mole Man | `dungeon.mole-man` | sip | A man who lives down here sells whatever washes past. He takes drinks, or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Rat Pharmacist | `dungeon.rat-pharmacist` | sip | A rat in a white coat with a briefcase of pills, potions and one very old book. |  |
| ☐ | Message in a Bottle | `dungeon.message-bottle` | sip | It says drink me. Of course it does. Drink 1, and keep the bottle. |  |
| ☐ | The Lifebuoy | `dungeon.lifebuoy` | sip | A lifebuoy off the wall, still inflated. Wear it with pride. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Pearl Diver | `dungeon.pearl-diver` | sip | A diver in a rusted helmet trades whatever he finds at the bottom of the bar. |  |
| ☐ | The Ferryman | `dungeon.the-ferryman` | sip | A hooded figure in a pedal boat takes you across, for a price, and sells extras. |  |
| ☐ | Staff Meal | `dungeon.staff-meal` | sip | Fries, pickles, and a shot the cooks hid for you. Drink 1, and pocket a smoke bomb. |  |
| ☐ | Knife Roll | `dungeon.knife-roll` | sip | A chef's knife roll left on the pass, something heavy in the end pocket. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Dishwasher | `dungeon.the-dishwasher` | sip | The dishwasher has seen everything, and he'll sell you some of it, for drinks or blood. |  |
| ☐ | The Sous Chef | `dungeon.the-sous-chef` | sip | The sous chef is selling the head chef's stash, quietly, out the back. |  |
| ☐ | Bottle Cap Stash | `dungeon.bottle-cap-stash` | sip | A jar of bottle caps, and a loaded die someone hid in it. Drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | The Growler | `dungeon.the-growler` | sip | A full growler on a shelf, sealed, labeled YOURS. Drink 1, and keep it. |  |

| ☐ | The Yeti | `dungeon.the-yeti` | sip | A yeti in a cardigan runs a little shop out of the ice cream aisle. |  |
| ☐ | The Frozen Mime | `dungeon.the-frozen-mime` | sip | A mime who got stuck in here mid-act. He sells by pointing. |  |

| ☐ | Candlelit Alcove | `dungeon.candlelit-alcove` | group | Someone left candles and a case of water down here. Everyone drinks water. Yes, really. |  |
| ☐ | The Ossuary Bar | `dungeon.the-ossuary-bar` | group | A bar made of bones, closed for centuries, with one last round. Everyone toasts the dead. |  |
| ☐ | The Split | `dungeon.the-split` | group | The tunnel splits. Left: silence. Right: drums, chanting, maybe a conga line. |  |
| ☐ | Two Crypts | `dungeon.two-crypts` | group | Two crypt doors. One says REST. The other says PARTY, in fresh blood. |  |
| ☐ | Storm Drain Skylight | `dungeon.storm-drain-skylight` | group | A grate overhead and real moonlight. Everyone looks up and makes a wish. |  |
| ☐ | The Dry Ledge | `dungeon.dry-ledge` | group | A dry ledge, a cooler and two lawn chairs. Everyone sits back and takes one slow sip. |  |
| ☐ | Two Pipes | `dungeon.two-pipes` | group | Two pipes. One drips. The other gurgles, and it sounds like laughing. |  |
| ☐ | The Overflow | `dungeon.the-overflow` | group | A dry service tunnel, or the overflow pipe, where something big just splashed. |  |
| ☐ | The Air Pocket | `dungeon.air-pocket` | group | A pocket of air and a jukebox that still works. Everyone sings a chorus, any chorus. |  |
| ☐ | The Last Dry Booth | `dungeon.dry-booth` | group | One booth the water never reached. Sit. Breathe. Everyone toasts dry socks. |  |
| ☐ | Two Taps | `dungeon.two-taps` | group | Two taps. One pours water. The other pours something that screams. |  |
| ☐ | The Trapdoor | `dungeon.the-trapdoor` | group | The cellar stairs, or a trapdoor behind the bar with bubbles coming up through it. |  |
| ☐ | The Break Room | `dungeon.break-room` | group | A couch, a microwave, and twelve minutes of peace. Everyone sits down and sips. |  |
| ☐ | Family Meal | `dungeon.family-meal` | group | The cooks sit down to eat, and they set a place for you. Everyone toasts the cooks. |  |
| ☐ | The Pass | `dungeon.the-pass` | group | Two ways out: the quiet back door, or straight through the line in the dinner rush. |  |
| ☐ | The Dumbwaiter | `dungeon.the-dumbwaiter` | group | The fire stairs, or the dumbwaiter, which is shaking and going down on its own. |  |
| ☐ | The Beer Garden | `dungeon.beer-garden` | group | A courtyard, fairy lights and long tables. Everyone toasts their left neighbor. |  |
| ☐ | Brewer's Break | `dungeon.brewers-break` | group | Pretzels, mustard and one working chair. Everyone eats a pretend pretzel and sips. |  |
| ☐ | Two Vats | `dungeon.two-vats` | group | Two vats to climb through. One is empty. The other bubbles and hums. |  |
| ☐ | The Conveyor Belt | `dungeon.conveyor-belt` | group | Duck under the bottling line, or ride the belt into the noisy room. |  |
| ☐ | The Compressor | `dungeon.the-compressor` | group | Warm air blowing out of a vent. Everyone huddles in and sips. |  |
| ☐ | Hot Chocolate | `dungeon.hot-chocolate` | group | A thermos of hot chocolate, still warm, with a note: FOR WHOEVER'S NEXT. Everyone sips, slowly. |  |
| ☐ | Two Shelves | `dungeon.two-shelves` | group | Behind the frozen peas, or through the ice cream aisle, where something licks. |  |
| ☐ | The Meat Hooks | `dungeon.the-meat-hooks` | group | Duck under the empty hooks, or push through the full ones, swinging on their own. |  |
| ☐ | The View | `dungeon.the-view` | group | The whole city, lit up below. Everyone stops, looks, and drinks to it. |  |
| ☐ | The Fire Pit | `dungeon.the-fire-pit` | group | A fire pit and a ring of chairs. Everyone sits closer than they need to and sips. |  |
| ☐ | Two Terraces | `dungeon.the-two-terraces` | group | The quiet terrace with fairy lights, or the loud one with the private party. |  |
| ☐ | The Open Window | `dungeon.the-fire-escape` | group | Take the stairs across, or climb through the open window, where someone is shouting. |  |
| ☐ | The Stairwell | `dungeon.landing-stairwell` | group | A cold stairwell and one flickering bulb. Everyone stretches. Level up, or sit down. |  |
| ☐ | The Smoking Area | `dungeon.landing-smoking-area` | group | Fresh air, a dead heater, somebody's vape. Everyone names their best moment so far. |  |
| ☐ | The Coat Check | `dungeon.landing-coat-check` | group | An empty coat check. Everyone names the coat they'd steal. Level up, or nap on the pile. |  |
| ☐ | The Bathroom Line | `dungeon.landing-bathroom-line` | group | Twelve deep, and everyone's best friends now. Everyone hypes their left neighbor. |  |
| ☐ | The Boiler Room | `dungeon.landing-boiler-room` | group | Warm pipes and somebody's cot. Everyone warms their hands. Level up, or lie down. |  |
| ☐ | A Campfire | `dungeon.landing-campfire` | group | Someone lit a fire in a keg. Everyone tells the scariest thing that's happened tonight. |  |
| ☐ | The Staff Room | `dungeon.landing-staff-room` | group | A kettle and a sign: WASH YOUR OWN MUG. Everyone mimes washing one. |  |
| ☐ | The Fire Escape | `dungeon.landing-fire-escape` | group | Stars, a city, and the street far below. Everyone toasts how far you've come. |  |
| ☐ | Gelatinous Shot | `dungeon.gelatinous-shot` | challenge | A wobbling cube of jello shots. Something's floating in it. A tooth? Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It engulfs you. Drink 6, no hands. 2-6: It engulfs you. Drink 3, no hands. 7-19: You eat your way out. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You eat the whole thing. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Rats in a Coat | `dungeon.rat-trench-coat` | challenge | Nine rats in a trench coat are trying to order a drink. Card them. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Their ID is better than yours. Drink 6. 2-6: Their ID is better than yours. Drink 3. 7-19: Bounced. Give 3 to whoever looks most like a rat in a coat. 20-20: Natural 20! Bounced. Give 6 to whoever looks most like a rat in a coat. |
| ☐ | Mold Elemental | `dungeon.mold-elemental` | challenge | The thing growing behind the ice machine has feelings and a mouth. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It moves into your lungs. Drink 6 and cough on both neighbors. 2-6: You breathe it in. Drink 3 and cough on your left neighbor. 7-19: Bleach. So much bleach. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! Bleach. So much bleach. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Drunk Goblin | `dungeon.drunk-goblin` | challenge | A goblin who's had nine. He wants to fight you, then hug you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He wins both. Drink 6 and hug your right neighbor. 2-6: He wins both. Drink 3 and hug your right neighbor. 7-19: He passes out on you. Loot his pockets. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! He passes out on you. Loot his pockets. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Skeleton Barback | `dungeon.skeleton-barback` | challenge | He's been restocking limes since 1974. Ask for a fresh one and roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He throws one at your eye. Drink 6. 2-6: He throws one at your eye. Drink 3. 7-19: He finds one. It's perfect. Give 3, with a lime if there is one. 20-20: Natural 20! He finds one. It's perfect. Give 6, with a lime if there is one. |
| ☐ | Possessed Mop | `dungeon.possessed-mop` | challenge | The mop is moving on its own and it's coming for your shoes. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: It mops your face. Drink 3. 2-2: It mops your drink. Drink 3. 3-3: You trip it. Give 2. 4-4: You snap the handle. Give 3. 5-5: You wring it out over the sink. Give 3. 6-6: You ride it like a witch's broom. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | Keg Spider | `dungeon.keg-spider` | challenge | Eight legs, eight beers, zero manners. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Bitten. Drink 6, and do a spider walk to the fridge and back. 2-6: Bitten. Drink 3, and do a spider walk to the fridge and back. 7-19: Squished. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! Squished, and its web was full of coasters. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Tab Goblin | `dungeon.tab-goblin` | challenge | It's been putting drinks on your tab since 2019. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It collects. Drink 8. 2-6: It collects. Drink 4. 7-19: You stiff it. Give 3 to whoever owes you money. 20-20: Natural 20! You stiff it. Give 6 to whoever owes you money. |
| ☐ | Goblin Shaman | `dungeon.goblin-shaman` | challenge | A goblin in a bucket hat waves a burnt sage stick at you and chants. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The chant works. Drink 6. 2-6: The chant works. Drink 3. 7-19: You blow out the sage. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You blow out the sage. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Cask Bat | `dungeon.cask-bat` | challenge | A bat lives in the empty cask and it hates the lights. Roll 2d6. | 2d6 — 2-5: It gets tangled in your hair. Drink 3. 6-12: You shoo it into a bucket. Give 3. |
| ☐ | Clipboard Troll | `dungeon.clipboard-troll` | challenge | You're not on the list. Walk heel to toe across the room and back. Wobble: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Riddling Tap | `dungeon.riddling-tap` | challenge | A tap with a face. Your left neighbor asks a riddle: 10 seconds, or drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Stuck Door | `dungeon.stuck-door` | challenge | Brace it shut with your back: wall-sit for 30 seconds. Stand early: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Keg Stand-Off | `dungeon.keg-stand-off` | challenge | Staring contest with your right neighbor. You blink or laugh first: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Sticky Floor | `dungeon.sticky-floor` | challenge | Your shoes are glued to forty years of spilled lager. Roll d20 to pull free. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You leave a shoe behind. Drink 4, and stay one shoe down until your next turn. 2-8: You leave a shoe behind. Drink 2, and stay one shoe down until your next turn. 9-19: You peel free. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You peel free. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Tripwire | `dungeon.christmas-lights` | challenge | Someone strung Christmas lights across the stairs at shin height. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You eat the stairs. Drink 4. 2-8: You eat the stairs. Drink 2. 9-19: You hop it. Give 2 to the clumsiest player here. 20-20: Natural 20! You hop it. Give 4 to the clumsiest player here. |
| ☐ | Rolling Kegs | `dungeon.rolling-kegs` | challenge | The keg rack groans. Then it lets go. Roll d20 to dive. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Flattened. Drink 6. 2-8: Flattened. Drink 3. 9-19: You dive clear. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You dive clear. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Loose Tap Handles | `dungeon.falling-taps` | challenge | A rack of novelty tap handles is right above your head. It's swaying. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! A plastic shark to the skull. Drink 4. 2-8: A plastic shark to the skull. Drink 2. 9-19: You catch the shark. Keep it. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You catch the shark. Keep it. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Lost Bridesmaids | `dungeon.lost-bridesmaids` | challenge | Two lost bridesmaids have decided you're the groom. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! First dance. Drink 4, then sway with your right neighbor. 2-6: First dance. Drink 2, then sway with your right neighbor. 7-19: You point them upstairs. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You point them upstairs. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Rat Pack | `dungeon.rat-pack` | challenge | Three rats in fedoras, singing Sinatra, and they want your stool. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They do it their way. Drink 4. 2-6: They do it their way. Drink 2. 7-19: You heckle them offstage. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You heckle them offstage. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Grease Trap Horror | `dungeon.grease-trap-horror` | challenge | The grease trap has been fed fries for twenty years. Now it's hungry. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It slurps you in. Drink 8, then wash your hands in shame. 2-9: It slurps you in. Drink 4, then wash your hands in shame. 10-19: You seal the lid and sit on it. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You seal the lid and sit on it. Give 8. |
| ☐ | The Health Inspector | `dungeon.health-inspector` | challenge | Clipboard, hairnet, no soul. He's here to shut the cellar down. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Violation. Drink 8 and name three things wrong with this room. 2-9: Violation. Drink 4 and name three things wrong with this room. 10-19: You bribe him with a keg. He leaves his lucky pen. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You bribe him with a keg. He leaves his lucky pen. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Keg Mimic Queen | `dungeon.keg-mimic-queen` | challenge | Every keg in the rack opens one eye. The big one in the middle opens two. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The whole rack bites. Drink 8. 2-9: The whole rack bites. Drink 4. 10-19: You tap the queen. The rack goes quiet. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You tap the queen. The rack goes quiet. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Keg Golem | `dungeon.keg-golem` | challenge | It's 800 pounds of lager and it's been tapped. Everyone drinks 1, then you roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It sits on you. Drink 8. 2-8: It sits on you. Drink 4. 9-19: You pull its plug. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You drink it dry. Everyone else drinks 3. |
| ☐ | The Rat King | `dungeon.rat-king` | challenge | Forty rats in one crown, holding court by the drain. Kiss the ring, then roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! The court finds you delicious. Finish your drink. 2-8: The court finds you guilty. Drink 4. 9-19: You dethrone him. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You dethrone him. Give 10. |
| ☐ | The Landlord | `dungeon.the-landlord` | challenge | He wants three months' rent and he's taking it out of your drink. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Evicted. Finish your drink. 2-8: Late fee. Drink 4. 9-19: You find a code violation. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You find a code violation. Give 10. |
| ☐ | Big Cheese | `dungeon.big-cheese` | challenge | A rat the size of a fridge, wearing a cheese wheel as armor. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It sits on your snacks. Drink 8. 2-8: It sits on your snacks. Drink 4. 9-19: You eat through the armor. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You eat the armor and the rat. Everyone else drinks 3. |
| ☐ | The Pipe Organ | `dungeon.the-pipe-organ` | challenge | Every pipe in the cellar is playing one chord, and it's coming from a face. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You join the chord. Hum for 10 seconds, then drink 8. 2-8: The chord hits you in the teeth. Drink 4. 9-19: You find the off switch. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You find the off switch. Give 10. |
| ☐ | Boxed Wine Golem | `dungeon.boxed-wine-golem` | challenge | A golem made of boxed wine, slapping its own bag. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It slaps you with the bag. Drink 6, and slap the table twice. 2-6: It slaps you with the bag. Drink 3, and slap the table twice. 7-19: You pop the spout. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You drink the bag dry. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Cork Imp | `dungeon.cork-imp` | challenge | A tiny imp popping corks at eye level and laughing about it. Roll d6. | 1d6 — 1-1: Right in the eye. Drink 3 with one eye shut. 2-2: Right in the drink. Drink 3. 3-3: You duck. Give 2. 4-4: You cork him. Give 3. 5-5: You cork him and shake him. Give 3. 6-6: You pop one back at him. Everyone else drinks 1. |
| ☐ | Zombie Sommelier | `dungeon.zombie-sommelier` | challenge | Brains? No. He wants to describe the finish. It takes eleven minutes. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Notes of oak. Drink 6 and describe the finish of yours. 2-6: Notes of oak. Drink 3 and describe the finish of yours. 7-19: You spit in his bucket. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You spit in his bucket. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Wine Mom Banshee | `dungeon.wine-mom-banshee` | challenge | She screams that it's five o'clock somewhere, and she means here. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! She tells you about her divorce. All of it. Drink 6. 2-6: It's five o'clock. Drink 3. 7-19: You take her glass away. Give 3 to the biggest wine mom here. 20-20: Natural 20! You take her glass away. Give 6 to the biggest wine mom here. |
| ☐ | Rosé Slime | `dungeon.rose-slime` | challenge | Pink, sticky, and it says it's not like other slimes. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It's exactly like other slimes. Drink 6. 2-6: It's exactly like other slimes. Drink 3. 7-19: You pop it. It leaves pink smoke behind. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You pop it. It leaves pink smoke behind. Give 4. |
| ☐ | Ghost in the Decanter | `dungeon.decanter-ghost` | challenge | It's been breathing for a hundred years. So has the wine. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! It breathes on you. Drink 6. 2-6: It breathes on you. Drink 3. 7-19: You jam the stopper in. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You jam the stopper in. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Barrel Mimic | `dungeon.barrel-mimic` | challenge | Barrel 14 has teeth. Barrel 15 looks nervous. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Barrel 14 wins. Drink 8. 2-6: Barrel 14 wins. Drink 4. 7-19: You tap it. It whimpers. Give 3 to the most nervous-looking person. 20-20: Natural 20! You tap it. It whimpers. Give 6 to the most nervous-looking person. |
| ☐ | Corkscrew Crab | `dungeon.corkscrew-crab` | challenge | A crab with corkscrews for claws, opening anything. Including you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Uncorked. Drink 6 and walk sideways to your seat. 2-6: Uncorked. Drink 3 and walk sideways to your seat. 7-19: You flip it on its back. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You open a bottle with it. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Sangria Sprite | `dungeon.sangria-sprite` | challenge | A sprite made of fruit chunks and bad decisions throws an orange slice at you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Fruit to the face. Drink 6. 2-6: Fruit to the face. Drink 3. 7-19: You eat the sprite. It was mostly brandy. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You eat the sprite. It was mostly brandy. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Vinegar Vampire | `dungeon.vinegar-vampire` | challenge | A vampire who only drinks from bottles that turned. He's been at yours. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He's right, it turned. Drink 6, make the face. 2-6: He's right, it turned. Drink 3, make the face. 7-19: You hold up a cross made of corkscrews. Give 3. 20-20: Natural 20! You hold up a cross made of corkscrews. Give 6. |
| ☐ | Blind Tasting | `dungeon.blind-tasting` | challenge | Eyes shut. Your left neighbor hands you a drink. Name whose it is. Wrong: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Flamingo | `dungeon.coaster-nose` | challenge | Stand on one leg with your eyes shut for 10 seconds. Wobble or peek: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | Swirl, Sniff, Lie | `dungeon.swirl-sniff-lie` | challenge | Review your drink like a sommelier for 15 seconds. Crack a smile: drink 3. |  |
| ☐ | The Toast | `dungeon.the-toast` | challenge | Toast your right neighbor, sincerely, for 20 seconds. Anyone laughs: you drink 3. |  |

| ☐ | Cork Mines | `dungeon.cork-mines` | challenge | The floor is covered in corks. Some of them click. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Pop. Drink 4. 2-8: Pop. Drink 2. 9-19: You tiptoe through. Give 2 to whoever walks loudest. 20-20: Natural 20! You tiptoe through. Give 4 to whoever walks loudest. |
| ☐ | Fruit Fly Swarm | `dungeon.fruit-fly-swarm` | challenge | A cloud of fruit flies off the dump bucket, and they brought friends. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! In your mouth. Drink 4 with a hand over your glass. 2-6: In your mouth. Drink 2 with a hand over your glass. 7-19: Swatted. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! Swatted. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Book Club | `dungeon.book-club` | challenge | Four women with one book none of them read, and a lot of opinions about you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! They have notes. Drink 4. 2-6: They have notes. Drink 2. 7-19: You've read it. They scatter. Give 2. 20-20: Natural 20! You've read it. They scatter. Give 4. |
| ☐ | The Wine Snob | `dungeon.wine-snob` | challenge | He swirls, he sniffs, he says you'd like something approachable. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Approachable. Drink 8, and say it's 'fun'. 2-9: Approachable. Drink 4, and say it's 'fun'. 10-19: You pour his '82 into a Solo cup. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You pour his '82 into a Solo cup. Give 8. |

Drink 8, then stomp your feet for 5 seconds. 2-9: Stomped. Drink 4, then stomp your feet for 5 seconds. 10-19: Raisin. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Raisin. Give 8. |

Drink 4. 10-19: You blame the cat. He believes you. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You blame the cat. He believes you. Give 8. |
| ☐ | Count Corkula | `dungeon.count-corkula` | challenge | He does not drink... blood. He drinks pinot. Yours. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He drains your glass. Finish your drink. 2-8: Your palate is basic. Drink 4. 9-19: You knock out a barrel. She tumbles. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You knock out a barrel. She tumbles. Give 10. |
| ☐ | Château Ghoul | `dungeon.chateau-ghoul` | challenge | A ghoul in a beret who thinks your wine is beneath you. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He sneers you to death. Drink 8. 2-8: He sneers you to death. Drink 4. 9-19: You hand him a White Claw. He dissolves. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! He dissolves into a nice rosé. Everyone else drinks 3. |
| ☐ | The Grand Sommelier | `dungeon.the-sommelier` | challenge | Ten feet tall, a tastevin on a chain, and he has never been wrong. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! He spits your drink back in your glass. Drink 8. 2-8: He names your drink, your year and your ex. Drink 4. 9-19: He calls your boxed wine a Bordeaux. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! He calls your boxed wine a Bordeaux. Give 10. |
| ☐ | The Barrel Room | `dungeon.the-barrel-room` | challenge | Two hundred barrels, one of them alive, and they're all rolling. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! Rolled flat. Drink 8. 2-8: Rolled flat. Drink 4. 9-19: You find the live one and tap it. Give 5. 20-20: Natural 20! You surf a barrel out. Everyone else drinks 3. |
| ☐ | Bachelor Ghosts | `dungeon.bachelor-ghosts` | challenge | The ghosts of a 1998 bachelor party want one more round. Roll d20. | 1d20 — 1-1: Natural 1! You're in the round. Drink 8 and tell your worst wedding story. 2-8: You're in the round. Drink 4 and tell your worst wedding story. 9-19: You exorcise them with karaoke. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! Drink 8. 2-11: He drinks yours in front of you. Drink 4. 12-19: You carry the tray to your own table. Give 4. 20-20: Natural 20! You carry the tray, and Chad tips you. Everyone else drinks 3. |

| ☐ | The Lock-In | `dungeon.omen-lock-in` | rule | Locked in until sunrise. Every boss takes one more hit to fall. Have a loaded die for it. |  |

| ☐ | Butterfingers | `dungeon.omen-butterfingers` | rule | Everyone's already a bit gone. One satchel slot fewer, but you get two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | Blood Moon | `dungeon.omen-blood-moon` | rule | Blood moon. Monsters, elites and ambushes cost a heart more. Start with two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | Lefty Night | `dungeon.omen-lefty-night` | rule | Everyone drinks with their off hand all crawl. Caught using the other: drink 1. |  |
| ☐ | Karaoke Night | `dungeon.omen-karaoke-night` | rule | Before every boss swing, the drawer sings one line of anything. Refuse: drink 2. |  |
| ☐ | The Regulars | `dungeon.omen-the-regulars` | rule | The bartender knows you, and your tab. Everyone drinks 2. Start with two more hearts. |  |
| ☐ | Thin Walls | `dungeon.omen-thin-walls` | rule | Everyone can hear you. Every trial costs a heart more, but every heal heals one more. |  |
| ☐ | The Pregame | `dungeon.omen-cold-open` | rule | Everyone drinks 1 at the door. Start a heart short, with a loaded die and a shield. |  |
| ☐ | Barbarian | `dungeon.class-barbarian` | rule | Rage first. Questions never. +2 to boss d20s. Traps hurt 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | Rogue | `dungeon.class-rogue` | rule | Already has your wallet. +2 to trap and relic d20s. +1 satchel slot. Starts with a Smoke Bomb. |  |
| ☐ | Cleric | `dungeon.class-cleric` | rule | Blesses every drink, loudly. Every heal heals 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | Wizard | `dungeon.class-wizard` | rule | Reads the menu like a spellbook. −2 max hearts. Starts with 2 Loaded Dice. |  |
| ☐ | Bard | `dungeon.class-bard` | rule | Brought a guitar. Nobody asked. +1 max heart. Natural 20 on a 19 or 20. |  |
| ☐ | Paladin | `dungeon.class-paladin` | rule | Sworn to guard the table's drinks. +1 max heart. Starts with a Coaster Shield. |  |
| ☐ | Ranger | `dungeon.class-ranger` | rule | Tracks the bar by smell. +2 to monster d20s. Bosses hurt 1 more. |  |
| ☐ | Drunken Monk | `dungeon.class-monk` | rule | Starts drunk. Gets better. −1 max heart. Natural 1 on a 1 or 2. |  |
| ☐ | Necromancer | `dungeon.class-necromancer` | rule | Has died twice tonight already. −3 max hearts. Starts with a Second Wind. |  |
| ☐ | Alchemist | `dungeon.class-alchemist` | rule | Has a flask for every flask. +1 max heart. +1 satchel slot. Starts with 2 Healing Draughts. |  |
| ☐ | Warlock | `dungeon.class-warlock` | rule | Sold their soul for a better seat. −3 max hearts. +2 to every battle d20. |  |
| ☐ | The Plus-One | `dungeon.class-plus-one` | rule | Nobody knows who invited them. −3 max hearts. −1 to boss d20s. Every 20 is rerolled. |  |

## Notes for review

- Dice cards are ordinary cards with a `dice` definition (which dice plus how
  to read the total); the engine pauses the deck until the roll is resolved.
- Secret cards show the secret only while a player holds the card's plaque;
  a fuse burns for a hidden time in its range, a clock counts down in view,
  and the card can't be put aside until time is up.
- Rules target 90 characters / 18 words; the build warns above that and
  rejects over 100 characters / 20 words.
- Rank cards for 9 / 10 / Jack / Queen are intentionally covered by the
  existing Rhyme Time, Categories, Rulemaster and Questions Only cards.
